### 2. App Shell with Navigation

**apps/desktop/src/features/shell/AppShell.tsx**
```typescript
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Outlet, NavLink, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { clsx } from 'clsx';
import { 
  LayoutDashboard, 
  ShoppingCart, 
  Package, 
  Users, 
  FileText, 
  Settings,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  Bell,
  Wifi,
  WifiOff,
  Cloud,
  Moon,
  Sun,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '@genx/ui/Button';
import { Avatar, AvatarFallback } from '@genx/ui/Avatar';
import { Badge } from '@genx/ui/Badge';
import { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';
import { useConnectivity } from '@/hooks/useConnectivity';

const navItems = [
  { icon: LayoutDashboard, label: 'Dashboard', path: '/dashboard' },
  { icon: ShoppingCart, label: 'POS', path: '/pos' },
  { icon: Package, label: 'Products', path: '/products' },
  { icon: Users, label: 'Customers', path: '/customers' },
  { icon: FileText, label: 'Reports', path: '/reports' },
  { icon: Settings, label: 'Settings', path: '/settings' },
];

export function AppShell() {
  const [sidebarExpanded, setSidebarExpanded] = useState(true);
  const [darkMode, setDarkMode] = useState(false);
  const location = useLocation();
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const { status, syncStatus, pendingCount } = useConnectivity();

  // Dark mode toggle
  useEffect(() => {
    if (darkMode) {
      document.documentElement.classList.add('dark');
    } else {
      document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark');
    }
  }, [darkMode]);

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-bg overflow-hidden">
      {/* Sidebar */}
      <aside
        className={clsx(
          'flex flex-col border-r border-border bg-bg-elevated transition-all duration-300',
          sidebarExpanded ? 'w-60' : 'w-16'
        )}
      >
        {/* Logo */}
        <div className="flex h-14 items-center border-b border-border px-4">
          {sidebarExpanded ? (
            <span className="text-xl font-bold text-primary">GENX</span>
          ) : (
            <span className="text-xl font-bold text-primary">G</span>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Navigation */}
        <nav className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto py-4">
          {navItems.map((item) => (
            <NavLink
              key={item.path}
              to={item.path}
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                clsx(
                  'flex items-center px-4 py-3 mx-2 rounded-lg transition-colors',
                  isActive
                    ? 'bg-primary/10 text-primary font-medium'
                    : 'text-text-secondary hover:bg-bg-subtle hover:text-text',
                  !sidebarExpanded && 'justify-center'
                )
              }
            >
              <item.icon className="h-5 w-5" />
              {sidebarExpanded && (
                <span className="ml-3">{item.label}</span>
              )}
            </NavLink>
          ))}
        </nav>

        {/* Bottom Actions */}
        <div className="border-t border-border p-2 space-y-2">
          {/* Dark Mode Toggle */}
          <button
            onClick={() => setDarkMode(!darkMode)}
            className="flex items-center justify-center w-full h-9 rounded-md hover:bg-bg-subtle transition-colors"
          >
            {darkMode ? (
              <Sun className="h-5 w-5 text-text-secondary" />
            ) : (
              <Moon className="h-5 w-5 text-text-secondary" />
            )}
          </button>

          {/* Collapse Toggle */}
          <button
            onClick={() => setSidebarExpanded(!sidebarExpanded)}
            className="flex items-center justify-center w-full h-9 rounded-md hover:bg-bg-subtle transition-colors"
          >
            {sidebarExpanded ? (
              <ChevronLeft className="h-5 w-5 text-text-secondary" />
            ) : (
              <ChevronRight className="h-5 w-5 text-text-secondary" />
            )}
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {/* Main Content */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        {/* Header */}
        <header className="flex h-14 items-center justify-between border-b border-border bg-bg-elevated px-6">
          {/* Left: Branch Selector */}
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-4">
            <select className="text-sm font-medium text-text bg-transparent border-none focus:outline-none cursor-pointer">
              <option>📍 Main Branch</option>
              <option>Downtown Store</option>
              <option>Westside Outlet</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Right: Status & User */}
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-4">
            {/* Connectivity Status */}
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              {status === 'online' ? (
                <>
                  <Wifi className="h-4 w-4 text-online" />
                  {sidebarExpanded && <span className="text-sm text-text-secondary">Online</span>}
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <WifiOff className="h-4 w-4 text-offline" />
                  {sidebarExpanded && <span className="text-sm text-offline">Offline</span>}
                </>
              )}
              
              {syncStatus === 'syncing' && (
                <Cloud className="h-4 w-4 text-syncing animate-pulse" />
              )}
              {syncStatus === 'error' && (
                <Cloud className="h-4 w-4 text-error" />
              )}
              {pendingCount > 0 && (
                <Badge variant="info">{pendingCount}</Badge>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Notifications */}
            <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="relative">
              <Bell className="h-5 w-5" />
              {pendingCount > 0 && (
                <span className="absolute top-1 right-1 h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-error" />
              )}
            </Button>

            {/* User Profile */}
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 border-l border-border pl-4">
              <Avatar className="h-8 w-8">
                <AvatarFallback className="bg-primary text-white text-sm font-medium">
                  {user?.name?.charAt(0) || 'U'}
                </AvatarFallback>
              </Avatar>
              {sidebarExpanded && (
                <div className="flex flex-col">
                  <span className="text-sm font-medium text-text">{user?.name}</span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-text-secondary">{user?.role}</span>
                </div>
              )}
              <button
                onClick={logout}
                className="text-xs text-text-secondary hover:text-error transition-colors"
              >
                Sign out
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </header>

        {/* Content Area */}
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto bg-bg">
          <Outlet />
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

**apps/desktop/src/hooks/useAuth.ts**
```typescript
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { persist } from 'zustand/middleware';

interface User {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  name: string;
  role: 'owner' | 'manager' | 'cashier';
  tenantId: string;
}

interface AuthState {
  user: User | null;
  isAuthenticated: boolean;
  login: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => void;
}

export const useAuth = create<AuthState>()(
  persist(
    (set) => ({
      user: null,
      isAuthenticated: false,
      
      login: async (email: string, password: string) => {
        // TODO: Replace with actual API call
        // Mock authentication for Phase 2
        await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 500));
        
        const mockUser: User = {
          id: 'user-1',
          email,
          name: 'John Doe',
          role: 'owner',
          tenantId: 'tenant-1',
        };
        
        set({ user: mockUser, isAuthenticated: true });
      },
      
      logout: () => {
        set({ user: null, isAuthenticated: false });
      },
    }),
    {
      name: 'genx-auth',
    }
  )
);
```

**apps/desktop/src/hooks/useConnectivity.ts**
```typescript
import { create } from 'zustand';

type ConnectivityStatus = 'online' | 'offline';
type SyncStatus = 'idle' | 'syncing' | 'synced' | 'error';

interface ConnectivityState {
  status: ConnectivityStatus;
  syncStatus: SyncStatus;
  pendingCount: number;
  lastSyncedAt: number | null;
  updateStatus: (status: ConnectivityStatus) => void;
  updateSyncStatus: (status: SyncStatus) => void;
  setPendingCount: (count: number) => void;
}

export const useConnectivity = create<ConnectivityState>((set) => ({
  status: navigator.onLine ? 'online' : 'offline',
  syncStatus: 'idle',
  pendingCount: 0,
  lastSyncedAt: null,
  
  updateStatus: (status) => set({ status }),
  updateSyncStatus: (status) => set({ syncStatus: status }),
  setPendingCount: (count) => set({ pendingCount: count }),
}));

// Listen to online/offline events
if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
  window.addEventListener('online', () => {
    const store = useConnectivity.getState();
    store.updateStatus('online');
  });
  
  window.addEventListener('offline', () => {
    const store = useConnectivity.getState();
    store.updateStatus('offline');
  });
}
```

**database/local/schema.sql**
```sql
-- Core Tables for Phase 2

-- Tenants (local metadata)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tenants (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  local_db_path TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
);

-- Users
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  email TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  role TEXT NOT NULL,
  is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  deleted_at INTEGER
);

-- Categories
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS categories (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  parent_id TEXT,
  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  deleted_at INTEGER,
  FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(id),
  FOREIGN KEY (parent_id) REFERENCES categories(id)
);

-- Products
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS products (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  sku TEXT NOT NULL,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  barcode TEXT,
  category_id TEXT,
  cost_price REAL NOT NULL,
  selling_price REAL NOT NULL,
  wholesale_price REAL,
  stock INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  minimum_stock INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
  images TEXT,  -- JSON array
  metadata TEXT,  -- JSON object
  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  deleted_at INTEGER,
  FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(id),
  FOREIGN KEY (category_id) REFERENCES categories(id),
  UNIQUE(tenant_id, sku),
  UNIQUE(tenant_id, barcode)
);

-- Orders
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS orders (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  order_number TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  customer_id TEXT,
  subtotal REAL NOT NULL,
  discount REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  tax REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  total REAL NOT NULL,
  payment_method TEXT NOT NULL,
  status TEXT NOT NULL,
  notes TEXT,
  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(id),
  FOREIGN KEY (customer_id) REFERENCES customers(id)
);

-- Order Items
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS order_items (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  order_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  product_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  sku TEXT NOT NULL,
  quantity INTEGER NOT NULL,
  price REAL NOT NULL,
  discount REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  tax REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  total REAL NOT NULL,
  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders(id),
  FOREIGN KEY (product_id) REFERENCES products(id)
);

-- Customers
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS customers (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  phone TEXT,
  email TEXT,
  address TEXT,
  balance REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  credit_limit REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  deleted_at INTEGER,
  FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(id)
);

-- Outbox (sync)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS outbox (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  entity_type TEXT NOT NULL,
  entity_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  operation TEXT NOT NULL,
  payload TEXT NOT NULL,
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  last_attempt_at INTEGER,
  synced_at INTEGER,
  error TEXT,
  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(id)
);

-- Audit Log
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS audit_log (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  user_id TEXT,
  action TEXT NOT NULL,
  entity_type TEXT NOT NULL,
  entity_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  before_value TEXT,
  after_value TEXT,
  device_id TEXT,
  ip_address TEXT,
  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(id)
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_products_tenant ON products(tenant_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_products_sku ON products(tenant_id, sku);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_products_barcode ON products(tenant_id, barcode);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_products_category ON products(category_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_outbox_status ON outbox(status, created_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_orders_tenant ON orders(tenant_id, created_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_tenant ON audit_log(tenant_id, created_at);
```

***

### 4. Product Management Feature

**apps/desktop/src/features/products/Products.tsx**
```typescript
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Search, Plus, MoreVertical, Filter } from 'lucide-react';
import { clsx } from 'clsx';
import { Button } from '@genx/ui/Button';
import { Input } from '@genx/ui/Input';
import { Badge } from '@genx/ui/Badge';
import {
  Table,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
  TableHead,
  TableCell,
  TableBody,
} from '@genx/ui/Table';
import { useProducts } from '@/hooks/useProducts';

export function Products() {
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const { products, isLoading, error } = useProducts();

  const filteredProducts = products?.filter((product) => {
    const matchesSearch =
      product.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      product.sku.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase());
    
    const matchesCategory = selectedCategory
      ? product.categoryId === selectedCategory
      : true;
    
    return matchesSearch && matchesCategory;
  });

  return (
    <div className="p-6 space-y-6">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-text">Products</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary mt-1">
            Manage your product catalog
          </p>
        </div>
        <Button>
          <Plus className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
          Add Product
        </Button>
      </div>

      {/* Filters */}
      <div className="flex items-center space-x-4">
        <div className="relative flex-1 max-w-md">
          <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-text-muted" />
          <Input
            placeholder="Search products..."
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            className="pl-10"
          />
        </div>
        <Button variant="outline" size="sm">
          <Filter className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
          Category
        </Button>
      </div>

      {/* Table */}
      <div className="border border-border rounded-lg overflow-hidden">
        <Table>
          <TableHeader>
            <TableRow>
              <TableHead>Product</TableHead>
              <TableHead>SKU</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Category</TableHead>
              <TableHead className="text-right">Cost</TableHead>
              <TableHead className="text-right">Price</TableHead>
              <TableHead className="text-right">Stock</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Status</TableHead>
              <TableHead className="text-center">Actions</TableHead>
            </TableRow>
          </TableHeader>
          <TableBody>
            {isLoading ? (
              <TableRow>
                <TableCell colSpan={8} className="text-center py-12">
                  <div className="animate-pulse text-text-muted">Loading...</div>
                </TableCell>
              </TableRow>
            ) : error ? (
              <TableRow>
                <TableCell colSpan={8} className="text-center py-12 text-error">
                  Failed to load products
                </TableCell>
              </TableRow>
            ) : filteredProducts?.length === 0 ? (
              <TableRow>
                <TableCell colSpan={8} className="text-center py-12 text-text-muted">
                  No products found
                </TableCell>
              </TableRow>
            ) : (
              filteredProducts?.map((product) => (
                <TableRow key={product.id} className="hover:bg-bg-subtle">
                  <TableCell>
                    <div className="font-medium text-text">{product.name}</div>
                    {product.barcode && (
                      <div className="text-xs text-text-muted font-mono mt-0.5">
                        {product.barcode}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="font-mono text-sm">
                    {product.sku}
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell>
                    <Badge variant="default">{product.categoryId || 'Uncategorized'}</Badge>
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="text-right text-text-secondary">
                    ${product.costPrice.toFixed(2)}
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="text-right font-medium text-text">
                    ${product.sellingPrice.toFixed(2)}
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="text-right">
                    <span
                      className={clsx(
                        'font-medium',
                        product.stock <= product.minimumStock
                          ? 'text-error'
                          : 'text-text'
                      )}
                    >
                      {product.stock}
                    </span>
                    {product.stock <= product.minimumStock && (
                      <Badge variant="error" className="ml-2">Low</Badge>
                    )}
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell>
                    <Badge
                      variant={
                        product.status === 'active'
                          ? 'success'
                          : product.status === 'inactive'
                          ? 'warning'
                          : 'default'
                      }
                    >
                      {product.status}
                    </Badge>
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="text-center">
                    <Button variant="ghost" size="icon">
                      <MoreVertical className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </Button>
                  </TableCell>
                </TableRow>
              ))
            )}
          </TableBody>
        </Table>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### 5. Basic POS Feature

**apps/desktop/src/features/pos/POS.tsx**
```typescript
import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import { Search, Plus, Minus, Trash2, CreditCard, Banknote, Smartphone } from 'lucide-react';
import { clsx } from 'clsx';
import { Button } from '@genx/ui/Button';
import { Input } from '@genx/ui/Input';
import { Badge } from '@genx/ui/Badge';
import { useProducts } from '@/hooks/useProducts';
import type { Product } from '@genx/types/domain/product';

interface CartItem extends Product {
  quantity: number;
}

export function POS() {
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [cart, setCart] = useState<CartItem[]>([]);
  const [paymentMethod, setPaymentMethod] = useState<'cash' | 'card' | 'mobile'>('cash');
  const [cashReceived, setCashReceived] = useState('');
  const { products } = useProducts();

  const filteredProducts = products?.filter((product) => {
    const matchesSearch =
      product.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      product.sku.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase());
    
    const matchesCategory = selectedCategory
      ? product.categoryId === selectedCategory
      : true;
    
    return matchesSearch && matchesCategory;
  });

  const subtotal = cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.sellingPrice * item.quantity, 0);
  const tax = subtotal * 0.05;
  const total = subtotal + tax;
  const change = paymentMethod === 'cash' ? parseFloat(cashReceived || '0') - total : 0;

  const addToCart = useCallback((product: Product) => {
    setCart((prev) => {
      const existing = prev.find((item) => item.id === product.id);
      if (existing) {
        return prev.map((item) =>
          item.id === product.id ? { ...item, quantity: item.quantity + 1 } : item
        );
      }
      return [...prev, { ...product, quantity: 1 }];
    });
  }, []);

  const updateQuantity = (productId: string, delta: number) => {
    setCart((prev) =>
      prev
        .map((item) =>
          item.id === productId
            ? { ...item, quantity: Math.max(0, item.quantity + delta) }
            : item
        )
        .filter((item) => item.quantity > 0)
    );
  };

  const removeFromCart = (productId: string) => {
    setCart((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== productId));
  };

  const completeSale = async () => {
    // TODO: Implement actual sale creation
    alert(`Sale completed!\nTotal: $${total.toFixed(2)}\nPayment: ${paymentMethod}`);
    setCart([]);
    setCashReceived('');
  };

  // Keyboard shortcuts
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'F2') {
        e.preventDefault();
        document.getElementById('product-search')?.focus();
      }
      if (e.key === 'F4' && cart.length > 0) {
        e.preventDefault();
        completeSale();
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [cart, paymentMethod, cashReceived]);

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full">
      {/* Product Selection */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col p-4 border-r border-border">
        <Input
          id="product-search"
          placeholder="Search products... (F2)"
          value={searchQuery}
          onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
          className="mb-4"
        />

        <div className="flex space-x-2 mb-4 overflow-x-auto">
          <Button
            variant={!selectedCategory ? 'primary' : 'secondary'}
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => setSelectedCategory(null)}
          >
            All
          </Button>
          <Button
            variant={selectedCategory === 'beverages' ? 'primary' : 'secondary'}
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => setSelectedCategory('beverages')}
          >
            Beverages
          </Button>
          <Button
            variant={selectedCategory === 'bakery' ? 'primary' : 'secondary'}
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => setSelectedCategory('bakery')}
          >
            Bakery
          </Button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
            {filteredProducts?.map((product) => (
              <button
                key={product.id}
                onClick={() => addToCart(product)}
                disabled={product.stock === 0}
                className={clsx(
                  'p-4 border border-border rounded-lg hover:border-primary hover:shadow-sm transition-all text-left bg-bg-elevated',
                  product.stock === 0 && 'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed'
                )}
              >
                <div className="font-medium text-text mb-1">{product.name}</div>
                <div className="text-primary font-semibold">
                  ${product.sellingPrice.toFixed(2)}
                </div>
                {product.stock === 0 && (
                  <Badge variant="error" className="mt-2">
                    Out of stock
                  </Badge>
                )}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Cart */}
      <div className="w-96 flex flex-col bg-bg-subtle">
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4">
          {cart.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center text-text-muted py-12">
              Cart is empty
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {cart.map((item) => (
                <div
                  key={item.id}
                  className="flex items-center justify-between bg-bg-elevated p-3 rounded-lg border border-border"
                >
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <div className="font-medium text-text text-sm">{item.name}</div>
                    <div className="text-text-secondary text-xs">
                      ${item.sellingPrice.toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                    <Button
                      variant="outline"
                      size="sm"
                      onClick={() => updateQuantity(item.id, -1)}
                    >
                      <Minus className="h-3 w-3" />
                    </Button>
                    <span className="w-8 text-center font-medium text-sm">
                      {item.quantity}
                    </span>
                    <Button
                      variant="outline"
                      size="sm"
                      onClick={() => updateQuantity(item.id, 1)}
                    >
                      <Plus className="h-3 w-3" />
                    </Button>
                    <Button
                      variant="ghost"
                      size="icon"
                      onClick={() => removeFromCart(item.id)}
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4 text-error" />
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="border-t border-border p-4 bg-bg-elevated space-y-4">
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-text-secondary text-sm">
              <span>Subtotal</span>
              <span>${subtotal.toFixed(2)}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between text-text-secondary text-sm">
              <span>Tax (5%)</span>
              <span>${tax.toFixed(2)}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between text-lg font-semibold text-text pt-2 border-t border-border">
              <span>Total</span>
              <span>${total.toFixed(2)}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
            <Button
              variant={paymentMethod === 'cash' ? 'primary' : 'secondary'}
              onClick={() => setPaymentMethod('cash')}
              className="flex flex-col items-center py-3 h-auto"
            >
              <Banknote className="h-5 w-5 mb-1" />
              <span className="text-xs">Cash</span>
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant={paymentMethod === 'card' ? 'primary' : 'secondary'}
              onClick={() => setPaymentMethod('card')}
              className="flex flex-col items-center py-3 h-auto"
            >
              <CreditCard className="h-5 w-5 mb-1" />
              <span className="text-xs">Card</span>
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant={paymentMethod === 'mobile' ? 'primary' : 'secondary'}
              onClick={() => setPaymentMethod('mobile')}
              className="flex flex-col items-center py-3 h-auto"
            >
              <Smartphone className="h-5 w-5 mb-1" />
              <span className="text-xs">Mobile</span>
            </Button>
          </div>

          {paymentMethod === 'cash' && (
            <Input
              type="number"
              placeholder="Cash received"
              value={cashReceived}
              onChange={(e) => setCashReceived(e.target.value)}
            />
          )}

          <Button onClick={completeSale} disabled={cart.length === 0} className="w-full h-12">
            Complete Sale - ${total.toFixed(2)}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

***

### 6. Dashboard

**apps/desktop/src/features/dashboard/Dashboard.tsx**
```typescript
import { TrendingUp, AlertTriangle, Package, DollarSign } from 'lucide-react';
import { Card, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardContent } from '@genx/ui/Card';
import { Badge } from '@genx/ui/Badge';
import { useProducts } from '@/hooks/useProducts';

export function Dashboard() {
  const { products } = useProducts();

  const lowStockProducts = products?.filter(
    (p) => p.stock <= p.minimumStock && p.stock > 0
  );
  const outOfStockProducts = products?.filter((p) => p.stock === 0);

  return (
    <div className="p-6 space-y-6">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-text">Dashboard</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary mt-1">
            {new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
              weekday: 'long',
              year: 'numeric',
              month: 'long',
              day: 'numeric',
            })}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Metrics */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-6">
        <Card>
          <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between space-y-0 pb-2">
            <CardTitle className="text-sm font-medium text-text-secondary">
              Total Products
            </CardTitle>
            <Package className="h-4 w-4 text-text-muted" />
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-text">
              {products?.length || 0}
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary mt-1">
              Across all categories
            </p>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        <Card>
          <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between space-y-0 pb-2">
            <CardTitle className="text-sm font-medium text-text-secondary">
              Low Stock
            </CardTitle>
            <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4 text-warning" />
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-text">
              {lowStockProducts?.length || 0}
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary mt-1">
              Items need attention
            </p>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        <Card>
          <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between space-y-0 pb-2">
            <CardTitle className="text-sm font-medium text-text-secondary">
              Out of Stock
            </CardTitle>
            <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4 text-error" />
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-text">
              {outOfStockProducts?.length || 0}
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary mt-1">
              Critical items
            </p>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        <Card>
          <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between space-y-0 pb-2">
            <CardTitle className="text-sm font-medium text-text-secondary">
              Today's Sales
            </CardTitle>
            <DollarSign className="h-4 w-4 text-success" />
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-text">$0.00</div>
            <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary mt-1">
              <span className="text-success font-medium">↑ 0%</span> from yesterday
            </p>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>

      {/* Low Stock Alerts */}
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Low Stock Alerts</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          {lowStockProducts?.length === 0 && outOfStockProducts?.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center text-text-muted py-8">
              All products are well stocked
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {lowStockProducts?.map((product) => (
                <div
                  key={product.id}
                  className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-bg-subtle rounded-lg"
                >
                  <div>
                    <div className="font-medium text-text">{product.name}</div>
                    <div className="text-sm text-text-secondary">
                      SKU: {product.sku}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-4">
                    <div className="text-right">
                      <div className="text-sm text-text-secondary">Current</div>
                      <div className="font-medium text-warning">
                        {product.stock} / {product.minimumStock}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <Badge variant="warning">Low Stock</Badge>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}

              {outOfStockProducts?.map((product) => (
                <div
                  key={product.id}
                  className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-error-bg rounded-lg"
                >
                  <div>
                    <div className="font-medium text-text">{product.name}</div>
                    <div className="text-sm text-text-secondary">
                      SKU: {product.sku}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-4">
                    <div className="text-right">
                      <div className="text-sm text-text-secondary">Current</div>
                      <div className="font-medium text-error">0</div>
                    </div>
                    <Badge variant="error">Out of Stock</Badge>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### 7. Testing Setup

**vitest.config.ts** (root)
```typescript
import { defineConfig } from 'vitest/config';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import path from 'path';

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react()],
  test: {
    globals: true,
    environment: 'jsdom',
    setupFiles: ['./tests/setup.ts'],
    include: ['**/*.test.ts', '**/*.test.tsx'],
    coverage: {
      provider: 'v8',
      reporter: ['text', 'json', 'html'],
    },
  },
  resolve: {
    alias: {
      '@': path.resolve(__dirname, './apps/desktop/src'),
      '@genx': path.resolve(__dirname, './packages'),
    },
  },
});
```

```typescript
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { Button } from '@genx/ui/Button';

describe('Button', () => {
  it('renders children correctly', () => {
    render(<Button>Click me</Button>);
    expect(screen.getByRole('button')).toHaveTextContent('Click me');
  });

  it('applies primary variant by default', () => {
    render(<Button>Primary</Button>);
    const button = screen.getByRole('button');
    expect(button).toHaveClass('bg-primary');
  });

  it('applies disabled styles when disabled', () => {
    render(<Button disabled>Disabled</Button>);
    const button = screen.getByRole('button');
    expect(button).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it('shows loading state', () => {
    render(<Button isLoading>Loading</Button>);
    const button = screen.getByRole('button');
    expect(button).toBeDisabled();
    expect(button.querySelector('svg')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
```

**apps/desktop/src/App.tsx**
```typescript
import { Routes, Route } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AppShell } from './features/shell/AppShell';
import { Dashboard } from './features/dashboard/Dashboard';
import { POS } from './features/pos/POS';
import { Products } from './features/products/Products';
import { LoginPage } from './features/auth/LoginPage';
import { useAuth } from './hooks/useAuth';

function App() {
  const { isAuthenticated } = useAuth();

  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return <LoginPage />;
  }

  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route path="/" element={<AppShell />}>
        <Route index element={<Dashboard />} />
        <Route path="dashboard" element={<Dashboard />} />
        <Route path="pos" element={<POS />} />
        <Route path="products" element={<Products />} />
        <Route path="customers" element={<div className="p-6">Customers - Coming Soon</div>} />
        <Route path="reports" element={<div className="p-6">Reports - Coming Soon</div>} />
        <Route path="settings" element={<div className="p-6">Settings - Coming Soon</div>} />
      </Route>
    </Routes>
  );
}

export default App;
```

**apps/desktop/src/features/auth/LoginPage.tsx**
```typescript
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Button } from '@genx/ui/Button';
import { Input } from '@genx/ui/Input';
import { Card, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardContent } from '@genx/ui/Card';
import { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';

export function LoginPage() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('demo@genx.com');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('password');
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const { login } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');
    setIsLoading(true);

    try {
      await login(email, password);
      navigate('/dashboard');
    } catch (err) {
      setError('Invalid email or password');
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-bg-subtle p-4">
      <Card className="w-full max-w-md">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="text-2xl font-bold text-center">
            <span className="text-primary">GENX</span> POS
          </CardTitle>
          <p className="text-center text-text-secondary">
            Sign in to your account
          </p>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
            {error && (
              <div className="p-3 bg-error-bg text-error text-sm rounded-lg">
                {error}
              </div>
            )}

            <Input
              label="Email"
              type="email"
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="demo@genx.com"
              required
            />

            <Input
              label="Password"
              type="password"
              value={password}
              onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="••••••••"
              required
            />

            <Button type="submit" isLoading={isLoading} className="w-full">
              Sign In
            </Button>

            <div className="text-center text-sm text-text-secondary">
              Demo credentials: demo@genx.com / password
            </div>
          </form>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## Phase 2 Implementation Report

### ✅ Completed

1. **Design System**
   - CSS variables for all design tokens
   - Button, Input, Badge, Card, Table components
   - Light/dark mode support
   - Consistent spacing, colors, typography

2. **App Shell**
   - Collapsible sidebar with navigation
   - Header with connectivity indicators
   - User profile and logout
   - Dark mode toggle
   - Branch selector

3. **Authentication**
   - Login page
   - JWT-based auth (mock for Phase 2)
   - Protected routes
   - Zustand persistence

4. **Database Layer**
   - Complete SQLite schema
   - Type definitions for all entities
   - Migration strategy documented

5. **Product Management**
   - Product list with search and filters
   - Status badges (active/inactive/low stock)
   - Responsive table layout

6. **Basic POS**
   - Product grid with categories
   - Shopping cart
   - Payment method selection
   - Keyboard shortcuts (F2, F4)
   - Change calculation

7. **Dashboard**
   - Metric cards (products, low stock, out of stock)
   - Low stock alerts
   - Real-time data from products

8. **Testing**
   - Vitest configuration
   - Example component tests
   - Test setup with Testing Library

### 📁 Files Created

- **packages/ui/**: Complete component library foundation
- **apps/desktop/src/features/**: Shell, Auth, Dashboard, POS, Products
- **apps/desktop/src/hooks/**: useAuth, useConnectivity, useProducts
- **database/local/schema.sql**: Complete SQLite schema
- **packages/types/src/domain/**: TypeScript type definitions
- **tests/**: Testing configuration and examples

### 🚀 Running the App

```bash

pnpm install

# Start development server
pnpm dev:desktop

# Open browser to http://localhost:3000
# Login: demo@genx.com / password
```

### ⚠️ Known Limitations (Phase 2)

1. **Mock Data**: Products are hardcoded, not from database
2. **No Backend**: API not implemented yet
3. **No SQLite Integration**: Database layer ready but not connected
4. **No Sync Engine**: Interfaces defined, not implemented
5. **Limited Features**: Customers, Reports, Settings are placeholders

### 🎯 Phase 3 Recommendations

1. **Database Integration**: Connect to actual SQLite
2. **API Development**: Build cloud API with Fastify
3. **Sync Engine**: Implement outbox pattern
4. **Complete CRUD**: Full product management (create, edit, delete)
5. **Customer Management**: Add customer module
6. **Reports**: Basic sales reports
7. **Hardware**: Printer integration

***

**Status**: ✅ Phase 2 Complete

The application is now functional with a professional UI, working navigation, authentication flow, product listing, POS interface, and dashboard. Ready for Phase 3 to add real database operations and backend integration.



## Phase 3 Implementation

### 1. SQLite Database Layer for Desktop

**apps/desktop/src/lib/db.ts**
```typescript
import { Database } from '@tauri-apps/plugin-sql';
import { Product } from '@genx/types/domain/product';
import { Order } from '@genx/types/domain/order';

let dbInstance: Database | null = null;

export async function getDatabase(): Promise<Database> {
  if (!dbInstance) {
    dbInstance = await Database.load('sqlite:genx.db');
  }
  return dbInstance;
}

export async function initializeDatabase(): Promise<void> {
  const db = await getDatabase();
  
  // Create tables if not exist
  await db.execute(`
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tenants (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      name TEXT NOT NULL,
      local_db_path TEXT NOT NULL,
      created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
      updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
    )
  `);

  await db.execute(`
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS products (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
      sku TEXT NOT NULL,
      name TEXT NOT NULL,
      description TEXT,
      barcode TEXT,
      category_id TEXT,
      cost_price REAL NOT NULL,
      selling_price REAL NOT NULL,
      wholesale_price REAL,
      stock INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      minimum_stock INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
      images TEXT,
      metadata TEXT,
      created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
      updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
      deleted_at INTEGER,
      UNIQUE(tenant_id, sku),
      UNIQUE(tenant_id, barcode)
    )
  `);

  await db.execute(`
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS orders (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
      order_number TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
      customer_id TEXT,
      subtotal REAL NOT NULL,
      discount REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      tax REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      total REAL NOT NULL,
      payment_method TEXT NOT NULL,
      status TEXT NOT NULL,
      notes TEXT,
      created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
      updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
    )
  `);

  await db.execute(`
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS order_items (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      order_id TEXT NOT NULL,
      product_id TEXT NOT NULL,
      name TEXT NOT NULL,
      sku TEXT NOT NULL,
      quantity INTEGER NOT NULL,
      price REAL NOT NULL,
      discount REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      tax REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      total REAL NOT NULL,
      created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
      FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders(id)
    )
  `);

  await db.execute(`
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS outbox (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
      entity_type TEXT NOT NULL,
      entity_id TEXT NOT NULL,
      operation TEXT NOT NULL,
      payload TEXT NOT NULL,
      status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
      attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      last_attempt_at INTEGER,
      synced_at INTEGER,
      error TEXT,
      created_at INTEGER NOT NULL
    )
  `);

  await db.execute(`
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS audit_log (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
      user_id TEXT,
      action TEXT NOT NULL,
      entity_type TEXT NOT NULL,
      entity_id TEXT NOT NULL,
      before_value TEXT,
      after_value TEXT,
      device_id TEXT,
      ip_address TEXT,
      created_at INTEGER NOT NULL
    )
  `);

  // Create indexes
  await db.execute(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_products_tenant ON products(tenant_id)`);
  await db.execute(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_products_sku ON products(tenant_id, sku)`);
  await db.execute(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_outbox_status ON outbox(status, created_at)`);
}

// Product Repository
export const productRepo = {
  async findAll(tenantId: string): Promise<Product[]> {
    const db = await getDatabase();
    const rows = await db.select<any[]>(
      'SELECT * FROM products WHERE tenant_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY name',
      [tenantId]
    );
    return rows.map(mapToProduct);
  },

  async findById(tenantId: string, id: string): Promise<Product | null> {
    const db = await getDatabase();
    const row = await db.select<any[]>(
      'SELECT * FROM products WHERE id = ? AND tenant_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL',
      [id, tenantId]
    );
    return row.length > 0 ? mapToProduct(row[0]) : null;
  },

  async create(product: Product): Promise<void> {
    const db = await getDatabase();
    await db.execute(
      `INSERT INTO products (id, tenant_id, sku, name, description, barcode, category_id, cost_price, selling_price, wholesale_price, stock, minimum_stock, status, images, metadata, created_at, updated_at)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
      [
        product.id,
        product.tenantId,
        product.sku,
        product.name,
        product.description || null,
        product.barcode || null,
        product.categoryId || null,
        product.costPrice,
        product.sellingPrice,
        product.wholesalePrice || null,
        product.stock,
        product.minimumStock,
        product.status,
        product.images ? JSON.stringify(product.images) : null,
        product.metadata ? JSON.stringify(product.metadata) : null,
        product.createdAt,
        product.updatedAt,
      ]
    );
  },

  async update(product: Partial<Product> & { id: string; tenantId: string }): Promise<void> {
    const db = await getDatabase();
    const existing = await this.findById(product.tenantId, product.id);
    if (!existing) throw new Error('Product not found');

    const updated = { ...existing, ...product, updatedAt: Date.now() };
    
    await db.execute(
      `UPDATE products SET 
        sku = ?, name = ?, description = ?, barcode = ?, category_id = ?, 
        cost_price = ?, selling_price = ?, wholesale_price = ?, 
        stock = ?, minimum_stock = ?, status = ?, images = ?, metadata = ?, updated_at = ?
       WHERE id = ? AND tenant_id = ?`,
      [
        updated.sku,
        updated.name,
        updated.description || null,
        updated.barcode || null,
        updated.categoryId || null,
        updated.costPrice,
        updated.sellingPrice,
        updated.wholesalePrice || null,
        updated.stock,
        updated.minimumStock,
        updated.status,
        updated.images ? JSON.stringify(updated.images) : null,
        updated.metadata ? JSON.stringify(updated.metadata) : null,
        updated.updatedAt,
        updated.id,
        updated.tenantId,
      ]
    );
  },

  async delete(tenantId: string, id: string): Promise<void> {
    const db = await getDatabase();
    await db.execute(
      'UPDATE products SET deleted_at = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ? AND tenant_id = ?',
      [Date.now(), Date.now(), id, tenantId]
    );
  },

  async search(tenantId: string, query: string): Promise<Product[]> {
    const db = await getDatabase();
    const rows = await db.select<any[]>(
      `SELECT * FROM products 
       WHERE tenant_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL 
       AND (name LIKE ? OR sku LIKE ? OR barcode LIKE ?)
       ORDER BY name`,
      [tenantId, `%${query}%`, `%${query}%`, `%${query}%`]
    );
    return rows.map(mapToProduct);
  },
};

function mapToProduct(row: any): Product {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    tenantId: row.tenant_id,
    sku: row.sku,
    name: row.name,
    description: row.description,
    barcode: row.barcode,
    categoryId: row.category_id,
    costPrice: row.cost_price,
    sellingPrice: row.selling_price,
    wholesalePrice: row.wholesale_price,
    stock: row.stock,
    minimumStock: row.minimum_stock,
    status: row.status,
    images: row.images ? JSON.parse(row.images) : undefined,
    metadata: row.metadata ? JSON.parse(row.metadata) : undefined,
    createdAt: row.created_at,
    updatedAt: row.updated_at,
    deletedAt: row.deleted_at,
  };
}

// Order Repository
export const orderRepo = {
  async create(order: Order, items: Array<{ productId: string; name: string; sku: string; quantity: number; price: number; discount: number; tax: number; total: number }>): Promise<void> {
    const db = await getDatabase();
    
    await db.execute(
      `INSERT INTO orders (id, tenant_id, order_number, customer_id, subtotal, discount, tax, total, payment_method, status, notes, created_at, updated_at)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
      [
        order.id,
        order.tenantId,
        order.orderNumber,
        order.customerId || null,
        order.subtotal,
        order.discount,
        order.tax,
        order.total,
        order.paymentMethod,
        order.status,
        order.notes || null,
        order.createdAt,
        order.updatedAt,
      ]
    );

    for (const item of items) {
      await db.execute(
        `INSERT INTO order_items (id, order_id, product_id, name, sku, quantity, price, discount, tax, total, created_at)
         VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
        [
          crypto.randomUUID(),
          order.id,
          item.productId,
          item.name,
          item.sku,
          item.quantity,
          item.price,
          item.discount,
          item.tax,
          item.total,
          Date.now(),
        ]
      );
    }
  },

  async findByDateRange(tenantId: string, startDate: number, endDate: number): Promise<Order[]> {
    const db = await getDatabase();
    const rows = await db.select<any[]>(
      `SELECT * FROM orders 
       WHERE tenant_id = ? AND created_at BETWEEN ? AND ?
       ORDER BY created_at DESC`,
      [tenantId, startDate, endDate]
    );
    return rows.map(row => ({
      ...row,
      tenantId: row.tenant_id,
      orderNumber: row.order_number,
      customerId: row.customer_id,
      paymentMethod: row.payment_method,
    }));
  },
};

// Outbox Repository
export const outboxRepo = {
  async insert(entry: {
    id: string;
    tenantId: string;
    entityType: string;
    entityId: string;
    operation: 'create' | 'update' | 'delete';
    payload: Record<string, any>;
  }): Promise<void> {
    const db = await getDatabase();
    await db.execute(
      `INSERT INTO outbox (id, tenant_id, entity_type, entity_id, operation, payload, status, created_at)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'pending', ?)`,
      [
        entry.id,
        entry.tenantId,
        entry.entityType,
        entry.entityId,
        entry.operation,
        JSON.stringify(entry.payload),
        Date.now(),
      ]
    );
  },

  async getPending(tenantId: string, limit: number = 100): Promise<any[]> {
    const db = await getDatabase();
    return await db.select<any[]>(
      `SELECT * FROM outbox 
       WHERE tenant_id = ? AND status = 'pending' 
       ORDER BY created_at ASC 
       LIMIT ?`,
      [tenantId, limit]
    );
  },

  async markSynced(id: string): Promise<void> {
    const db = await getDatabase();
    await db.execute(
      `UPDATE outbox SET status = 'synced', synced_at = ? WHERE id = ?`,
      [Date.now(), id]
    );
  },

  async markFailed(id: string, error: string): Promise<void> {
    const db = await getDatabase();
    await db.execute(
      `UPDATE outbox SET status = 'failed', error = ?, attempts = attempts + 1, last_attempt_at = ? WHERE id = ?`,
      [error, Date.now(), id]
    );
  },
};
```

***

### 2. Sync Engine Implementation

**sync/src/SyncEngine.ts**
```typescript
import { OutboxEntry, SyncResult, SyncStatus } from './types';

export class SyncEngine {
  private apiBaseUrl: string;
  private tenantId: string;
  private isSyncing = false;
  private syncInterval: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;

  constructor(apiBaseUrl: string, tenantId: string) {
    this.apiBaseUrl = apiBaseUrl;
    this.tenantId = tenantId;
  }

  /**
   * Start background sync
   */
  start(intervalMs: number = 30000): void {
    if (this.syncInterval) {
      clearInterval(this.syncInterval);
    }

    this.syncInterval = setInterval(async () => {
      if (navigator.onLine && !this.isSyncing) {
        await this.sync();
      }
    }, intervalMs);

    // Initial sync
    this.sync();
  }

  /**
   * Stop background sync
   */
  stop(): void {
    if (this.syncInterval) {
      clearInterval(this.syncInterval);
      this.syncInterval = null;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Force immediate sync
   */
  async sync(): Promise<SyncResult> {
    if (this.isSyncing) {
      return { success: false, syncedCount: 0, failedCount: 0, errors: ['Sync in progress'] };
    }

    this.isSyncing = true;
    const result: SyncResult = {
      success: true,
      syncedCount: 0,
      failedCount: 0,
      errors: [],
    };

    try {
      // Push local changes
      const pushResult = await this.pushChanges();
      result.syncedCount += pushResult.synced;
      result.failedCount += pushResult.failed;
      result.errors.push(...pushResult.errors);

      // Pull remote changes
      const pullResult = await this.pullChanges();
      result.syncedCount += pullResult.synced;

      if (result.failedCount > 0) {
        result.success = false;
      }
    } catch (error) {
      result.success = false;
      result.errors.push(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error');
    } finally {
      this.isSyncing = false;
    }

    return result;
  }

  /**
   * Push pending outbox entries to cloud
   */
  private async pushChanges(): Promise<{ synced: number; failed: number; errors: string[] }> {
    const result = { synced: 0, failed: 0, errors: [] as string[] };

    // Get pending entries from local outbox
    const pendingEntries = await this.getPendingOutboxEntries();

    if (pendingEntries.length === 0) {
      return result;
    }

    const response = await fetch(`${this.apiBaseUrl}/api/v1/sync/push`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${await this.getAuthToken()}`,
        'X-Tenant-ID': this.tenantId,
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        changes: pendingEntries.map(entry => ({
          id: entry.id,
          entity_type: entry.entity_type,
          entity_id: entry.entity_id,
          operation: entry.operation,
          payload: JSON.parse(entry.payload),
          client_timestamp: entry.created_at,
        })),
      }),
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}: ${response.statusText}`);
    }

    const syncResponse = await response.json();

    // Mark synced entries
    for (const id of syncResponse.synced) {
      await this.markOutboxSynced(id);
      result.synced++;
    }

    // Mark failed entries
    for (const error of syncResponse.errors || []) {
      await this.markOutboxFailed(error.id, error.message);
      result.failed++;
      result.errors.push(error.message);
    }

    return result;
  }

  /**
   * Pull remote changes from cloud
   */
  private async pullChanges(): Promise<{ synced: number }> {
    const lastSyncTimestamp = await this.getLastSyncTimestamp();

    const response = await fetch(
      `${this.apiBaseUrl}/api/v1/sync/pull?since=${lastSyncTimestamp}`,
      {
        method: 'GET',
        headers: {
          'Authorization': `Bearer ${await this.getAuthToken()}`,
          'X-Tenant-ID': this.tenantId,
        },
      }
    );

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}: ${response.statusText}`);
    }

    const syncResponse = await response.json();

    // Apply remote changes to local database
    for (const change of syncResponse.changes || []) {
      await this.applyRemoteChange(change);
    }

    // Update last sync timestamp
    await this.setLastSyncTimestamp(syncResponse.last_timestamp);

    return { synced: syncResponse.changes?.length || 0 };
  }

  /**
   * Apply a remote change to local database
   */
  private async applyRemoteChange(change: any): Promise<void> {
    // TODO: Implement based on entity type
    // This will merge remote changes into local SQLite
    console.log('Applying remote change:', change);
  }

  /**
   * Get pending outbox entries
   */
  private async getPendingOutboxEntries(): Promise<any[]> {
    // This will be called from the app code with actual DB access
    // Placeholder for now
    return [];
  }

  /**
   * Mark outbox entry as synced
   */
  private async markOutboxSynced(id: string): Promise<void> {
    // Placeholder - will be implemented in app
    console.log('Marking synced:', id);
  }

  /**
   * Mark outbox entry as failed
   */
  private async markOutboxFailed(id: string, error: string): Promise<void> {
    // Placeholder
    console.log('Marking failed:', id, error);
  }

  /**
   * Get last sync timestamp
   */
  private async getLastSyncTimestamp(): Promise<number> {
    const stored = localStorage.getItem('genx:lastSyncTimestamp');
    return stored ? parseInt(stored, 10) : 0;
  }

  /**
   * Set last sync timestamp
   */
  private async setLastSyncTimestamp(timestamp: number): Promise<void> {
    localStorage.setItem('genx:lastSyncTimestamp', timestamp.toString());
  }

  /**
   * Get auth token
   */
  private async getAuthToken(): Promise<string> {
    const stored = localStorage.getItem('genx:authToken');
    return stored || '';
  }
}

export default SyncEngine;
```

**sync/src/types.ts**
```typescript
export interface OutboxEntry {
  id: string;
  tenant_id: string;
  entity_type: string;
  entity_id: string;
  operation: 'create' | 'update' | 'delete';
  payload: string;
  status: 'pending' | 'syncing' | 'synced' | 'failed';
  attempts: number;
  last_attempt_at?: number;
  synced_at?: number;
  error?: string;
  created_at: number;
}

export interface SyncResult {
  success: boolean;
  syncedCount: number;
  failedCount: number;
  errors: string[];
}

export interface SyncStatus {
  status: 'idle' | 'syncing' | 'synced' | 'error';
  lastSyncedAt: number | null;
  pendingCount: number;
}

export interface SyncChange {
  id: string;
  entity_type: string;
  entity_id: string;
  operation: 'create' | 'update' | 'delete';
  payload: Record<string, any>;
  client_timestamp: number;
}

export interface SyncPushRequest {
  changes: SyncChange[];
}

export interface SyncPushResponse {
  synced: string[];
  conflicts: Conflict[];
  errors: SyncError[];
}

export interface Conflict {
  id: string;
  entity_type: string;
  entity_id: string;
  local: Record<string, any>;
  remote: Record<string, any>;
  strategy: 'last-write-wins' | 'manual';
}

export interface SyncError {
  id: string;
  message: string;
}

export interface SyncPullResponse {
  changes: Record<string, any>[];
  deletes: string[];
  last_timestamp: number;
}
```

**sync/src/OutboxService.ts**
```typescript
import { OutboxEntry } from './types';

export class OutboxService {
  /**
   * Record a create operation in outbox
   */
  async recordCreate(
    tenantId: string,
    entityType: string,
    entityId: string,
    payload: Record<string, any>
  ): Promise<string> {
    const id = crypto.randomUUID();
    
    // Insert into outbox table
    await this.insertOutboxEntry({
      id,
      tenant_id: tenantId,
      entity_type: entityType,
      entity_id: entityId,
      operation: 'create',
      payload: JSON.stringify(payload),
      status: 'pending',
      attempts: 0,
      created_at: Date.now(),
    });

    return id;
  }

  /**
   * Record an update operation in outbox
   */
  async recordUpdate(
    tenantId: string,
    entityType: string,
    entityId: string,
    payload: Record<string, any>
  ): Promise<string> {
    const id = crypto.randomUUID();
    
    await this.insertOutboxEntry({
      id,
      tenant_id: tenantId,
      entity_type: entityType,
      entity_id: entityId,
      operation: 'update',
      payload: JSON.stringify(payload),
      status: 'pending',
      attempts: 0,
      created_at: Date.now(),
    });

    return id;
  }

  /**
   * Record a delete operation in outbox
   */
  async recordDelete(
    tenantId: string,
    entityType: string,
    entityId: string
  ): Promise<string> {
    const id = crypto.randomUUID();
    
    await this.insertOutboxEntry({
      id,
      tenant_id: tenantId,
      entity_type: entityType,
      entity_id: entityId,
      operation: 'delete',
      payload: JSON.stringify({ id: entityId }),
      status: 'pending',
      attempts: 0,
      created_at: Date.now(),
    });

    return id;
  }

  /**
   * Insert outbox entry (to be implemented with actual DB)
   */
  private async insertOutboxEntry(entry: OutboxEntry): Promise<void> {
    // This will be implemented in the app with actual database access
    console.log('Inserting outbox entry:', entry);
  }

  /**
   * Get pending entries count
   */
  async getPendingCount(tenantId: string): Promise<number> {
    // Query outbox table for pending entries
    return 0; // Placeholder
  }
}

export default OutboxService;
```

**apps/api/src/index.ts**
```typescript
import Fastify from 'fastify';
import cors from '@fastify/cors';
import jwt from '@fastify/jwt';
import { authRoutes } from './routes/auth';
import { syncRoutes } from './routes/sync';
import { productRoutes } from './routes/products';

const server = Fastify({
  logger: {
    level: process.env.LOG_LEVEL || 'info',
  },
});

// Register plugins
await server.register(cors, {
  origin: true,
  credentials: true,
});

await server.register(jwt, {
  secret: process.env.JWT_SECRET || 'fallback-secret-change-in-production',
  sign: {
    expiresIn: '1h',
  },
});

// Register routes
await server.register(authRoutes);
await server.register(syncRoutes);
await server.register(productRoutes);

// Health check
server.get('/health', async (request, reply) => {
  return { status: 'ok', timestamp: Date.now() };
});

// Start server
const port = parseInt(process.env.API_PORT || '4000', 10);
const host = process.env.API_HOST || '0.0.0.0';

try {
  await server.listen({ port, host });
  console.log(`🚀 API server running at http://${host}:${port}`);
} catch (err) {
  server.log.error(err);
  process.exit(1);
}
```

**apps/api/src/routes/sync.ts**
```typescript
import { FastifyInstance, FastifyRequest, FastifyReply } from 'fastify';
import { z } from 'zod';

const syncPushSchema = z.object({
  changes: z.array(
    z.object({
      id: z.string(),
      entity_type: z.string(),
      entity_id: z.string(),
      operation: z.enum(['create', 'update', 'delete']),
      payload: z.record(z.unknown()),
      client_timestamp: z.number(),
    })
  ),
});

export async function syncRoutes(server: FastifyInstance) {
  // Push local changes to cloud
  server.post(
    '/sync/push',
    {
      schema: {
        body: syncPushSchema,
      },
      preHandler: [authenticate],
    },
    async (request: FastifyRequest<{ Body: z.infer<typeof syncPushSchema> }>, reply: FastifyReply) => {
      const tenantId = request.headers['x-tenant-id'] as string;
      const { changes } = request.body;

      const synced: string[] = [];
      const errors: Array<{ id: string; message: string }> = [];
      const conflicts: any[] = [];

      for (const change of changes) {
        try {
          await applyChange(tenantId, change);
          synced.push(change.id);
        } catch (error) {
          errors.push({
            id: change.id,
            message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error',
          });
        }
      }

      return reply.send({ synced, conflicts, errors });
    }
  );

  // Pull remote changes from cloud
  server.get(
    '/sync/pull',
    {
      preHandler: [authenticate],
    },
    async (request: FastifyRequest<{ Querystring: { since: string } }>, reply: FastifyReply) => {
      const tenantId = request.headers['x-tenant-id'] as string;
      const since = request.query.since ? parseInt(request.query.since, 10) : 0;

      // Get changes since timestamp
      const changes = await getRemoteChanges(tenantId, since);
      const deletes: string[] = [];
      const lastTimestamp = Date.now();

      return reply.send({ changes, deletes, last_timestamp: lastTimestamp });
    }
  );
}

async function applyChange(tenantId: string, change: any): Promise<void> {
  // TODO: Implement based on entity type
  // For now, just log
  console.log('Applying change:', change);
}

async function getRemoteChanges(tenantId: string, since: number): Promise<any[]> {
  // TODO: Query database for changes since timestamp
  return [];
}

async function authenticate(request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  try {
    await request.jwtVerify();
  } catch (error) {
    return reply.code(401).send({ error: 'Unauthorized' });
  }
}
```

**apps/api/src/routes/auth.ts**
```typescript
import { FastifyInstance, FastifyRequest, FastifyReply } from 'fastify';
import bcrypt from 'bcrypt';
import { z } from 'zod';

const loginSchema = z.object({
  email: z.string().email(),
  password: z.string().min(6),
});

export async function authRoutes(server: FastifyInstance) {
  server.post(
    '/auth/login',
    {
      schema: {
        body: loginSchema,
      },
    },
    async (request: FastifyRequest<{ Body: z.infer<typeof loginSchema> }>, reply: FastifyReply) => {
      const { email, password } = request.body;

      // TODO: Query database for user
      // Mock for Phase 3
      const mockUser = {
        id: 'user-1',
        email,
        name: 'John Doe',
        role: 'owner',
        tenantId: 'tenant-1',
      };

      // Generate JWT
      const token = await server.jwt.sign({
        sub: mockUser.id,
        email: mockUser.email,
        tenant_id: mockUser.tenantId,
        role: mockUser.role,
      });

      const refreshToken = await server.jwt.sign({
        sub: mockUser.id,
        type: 'refresh',
      }, { expiresIn: '7d' });

      return reply.send({
        user: mockUser,
        token,
        refreshToken,
      });
    }
  );

  server.post(
    '/auth/refresh',
    async (request: FastifyRequest<{ Body: { refreshToken: string } }>, reply: FastifyReply) => {
      const { refreshToken } = request.body;

      try {
        const decoded = await request.jwtVerify(refreshToken);
        
        const newToken = await server.jwt.sign({
          sub: decoded.sub,
          email: decoded.email,
          tenant_id: decoded.tenant_id,
          role: decoded.role,
        });

        return reply.send({ token: newToken });
      } catch (error) {
        return reply.code(401).send({ error: 'Invalid refresh token' });
      }
    }
  );
}
```

### 4. Integration with App

**apps/desktop/src/lib/sync.ts**
```typescript
import SyncEngine from '@genx/sync/SyncEngine';
import { outboxRepo } from './db';

const API_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || 'http://localhost:4000';

export const syncEngine = new SyncEngine(API_URL, 'tenant-1');

// Start sync when app loads
export function initializeSync(): void {
  syncEngine.start(30000); // Sync every 30 seconds
}

// Hook up outbox to sync
export async function recordProductChange(
  operation: 'create' | 'update' | 'delete',
  product: any
): Promise<void> {
  await outboxRepo.insert({
    id: crypto.randomUUID(),
    tenantId: product.tenantId,
    entityType: 'product',
    entityId: product.id,
    operation,
    payload: product,
  });

  // Trigger sync
  syncEngine.sync();
}
```

**apps/desktop/src/hooks/useProducts.ts** (Updated)
```typescript
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Product } from '@genx/types/domain/product';
import { productRepo, outboxRepo } from '@/lib/db';
import { recordProductChange } from '@/lib/sync';

export function useProducts() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['products'],
    queryFn: async (): Promise<Product[]> => {
      return await productRepo.findAll('tenant-1');
    },
    staleTime: 1000 * 60,
  });
}

export function useCreateProduct() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (product: Omit<Product, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'updatedAt'>) => {
      const newProduct: Product = {
        ...product,
        id: crypto.randomUUID(),
        createdAt: Date.now(),
        updatedAt: Date.now(),
      };

      // Save to local database
      await productRepo.create(newProduct);

      // Record in outbox for sync
      await outboxRepo.insert({
        id: crypto.randomUUID(),
        tenantId: newProduct.tenantId,
        entityType: 'product',
        entityId: newProduct.id,
        operation: 'create',
        payload: newProduct,
      });

      return newProduct;
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['products'] });
    },
  });
}
```
